         continue;\n        }\n\n        /* Parse message as JSON */\n        json_error_t jerr;\n        json_t *root = json_loadb((const char *)rkmsg->payload,\n                                  rkmsg->len, 0, &jerr);\n        if (!root) {\n            LOG_WARN(\"JSON parse error at line %d: %s\", jerr.line, jerr.text);\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* Validate schema */\n        json_t *type_js = json_object_get(root, \"type\");\n        json_t *payload = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n        json_t *sig     = line,\n                         const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    if (lvl < g_log_level) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n\n    struct tm != s)\n        memmove(s, p, strlen(p) + 1);\n}\n\n/* Extremely naive key search */\nstatic const char *json_get_value(const char *json, const char *key, char *buf, size_t buf_sz)\n{\n    char pattern[64];\n    snprintf(pattern, sizeof pattern, \"\\\"%s\\\":\\\"\", key);\n    const char *p = strstr(json, pattern);\n    if (!p) return NULL;\n    p += strlen(pattern);\n    const char *q = strchr(p, '\"');\n    if (!q) return NULL;\n    size_t len = (size_t)(q - p);\n    if (len >= buf_sz) len = buf_sz - 1;\n    memcpy(buf, p, len);\n    buf[len] = '\\0';\n    return buf;\n}\n\nstatic -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic void handle_event(const event_t *ev)\n{\n    governance_proposal_t *p = NULL;\n\n    switch (ev->type) {\n pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_prop_lock);\n\n        if (!p) {\n            LOGW(\"Finalize requested for unknown proposal %s\", ev->id);\n            break;\n        }\n        proposal_try_finalize(p);\n rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg =\n            rd_kafka_consumer_poll(listener->rk, 100 /* ms */);\n\n        if (!rkmsg)\n            continue; /* timeout – poll again */\n\n        if (rkmsg->err) {\n            if (rkmsg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n                LOG_DEBUG(\"Reached end of partition %\"PRId32\n                          \" at offset %\"PRId64,\n                          rkmsg->partition, rkmsg->offset);\n            } else {\n                LOG_WARN(\"Consume error: %s\",\n                         rd_kafka_message_errstr(rkmsg));\n            }\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* Dispatch user payload */\n        if (dispatch_event(listener,\n                           (const char *)rkmsg->payload,\n                           rkmsg->len) != 0) {\n            LOG_WARN(\"Failed to dispatch event (offset %\"PRId64\")\",\n                     rkmsg->offset);\n        }\n\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n    }\n\n    rd_kafka_consumer_close(listener->rk);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/* ----  Event dispatch ---------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic int dispatch_event(EventListener *listener,\n                          const char *payload, size_t len) {\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb(payload, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"JSON parse failed: %s (line %d)\", jerr.text, jerr.line);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    json_t *type = json_object_get(root, \"type\");\n    if (!json_is_string(type)) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Event missing string 'type' field\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    const char *etype = json_string_value(type);\n    LOG_INFO(\"Received event: %s\", etype);\n\n    /* Build a thin wrapper for plug-in */\n    MuseEvent evt = {\n        .raw_json = root,\n        .type     = etype,\n    };\n\n    int rc = listener->strategy->on_event(listener->strategy, &evt);\n    if (rc != 0)\n        LOG_WARN(\"Strategy returned non-zero (%d) for event %s\", rc, etype);\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return rc;\n}\n```\n","numLines":344,"startLine":1,"totalLines":344}}}

          char      **out_recipe_json);\n/* Optionally expose this (for init). */\ntypedef int (*strategy_init_fn)(void);\ntypedef void (*strategy_fini_fn)(void);\n\ntypedef struct {\n    char          name[MAX_PLUGIN_NAME];\n    void         *dl_handle;\n    strategy_fn   run;\n    strategy_fini_fn fini;\n} plugin_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Signal Handling                                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic void muse_on_signal(int signo) {\n    (void)signo;\n    g_terminate = 1;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Utility                                                                   */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic char *dup_or_default(const char *src, const char *def) {\n    return strdup(src && *src ? src : def);\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Command-line / Environment Parsing                                        */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic void muse_parse_cli(int argc, char **argv, muse_cfg_t *cfg) {\n    static struct option long_opts[] = {\n        { \"brokers\",     required_argument, 0, 'b' },\n        { \"topic\",       required_argument, 0, 't' },\n        { \"plugin-dir\",  required_argument, 0, 'p' },\n        { \"grpc\",        required_argument, 0, 'g' },\n        { \"verbose\",     no_argument,       0, 'v' },\n        { \"version\",     no_argument,       0, 'V' },\n        { \"help\",        no_argument,       0, 'h' },\n        { 0,             0,                 0,  0  }\n    };\n\n    int opt;\n    while ((opt = getopt_long(argc, argv, \"b:t:p:g:vVh\", long_opts, NULL)) != -1) {\n        switch (opt) {\n        case 'b': free(cfg->kafka_brokers); cfg->kafka_brokers = strdup(optarg); break;\n        case 't': free(cfg->topic);         cfg->topic        = strdup(optarg);  break;\n        case 'p': free(cfg->plugin_dir);    cfg->plugin_dir   = strdup(optarg);  break;\n        case 'g': free(cfg->grpc_target);   cfg->grpc_target  = strdup(optarg);  break;\n        case 'v': cfg->verbosity++;                                           break;\n        case 'V':\n            printf(\"Muse Observer v%s\\n\", MUSE_VERSION);\n            exit(EXIT_SUCCESS);\n        case 'h':\n        default:\n            printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\"\n                   \"  -b, --brokers    Kafka bootstrap servers (default: %s)\\n\"\n                   \"  -t, --topic      Kafka topic to consume   (default: %s)\\n\"\n                   \"  -p, --plugin-dir Directory containing strategy plug-ins\\n\"\n                   \"                   (default: %s)\\n\"\n                   \"  -g, --grpc       Mint-Factory gRPC target (default: %s)\\n\"\n                   \"  -v, --verbose    Increase verbosity (can repeat)\\n\"\n                   \"  -V, --version    Show version\\n\"\n                   \"  -h, --help       Show this help message\\n\",\n                   argv[0], DEFAULT_BROKERS, DEFAULT_TOPIC,\n                   DEFAULT_PLUGDIR, DEFAULT_GRPC_TGT);\n            exit(opt == 'h' ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Plug-in Loader                                                            */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int plugin_open(plugin_t *plg, const char *plugin_dir,\n                       const char *strategy_name)\n{\n    snprintf(plg->name, sizeof(plg->name), \"%s\", strategy_name);\n\n    char *so_path;\n    if (asprintf(&so_path, \"%s/lib%s.so\", plugin_dir, strategy_name) < 0)\n        return -1;\n\n    plg->dl_handle = dlopen(so_path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL);\n    free(so_path);\n\n    if (!plg->dl_handle) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"dlopen failed for strategy `%s`: %s\",\n            strategy_name, dlerror());\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* mandatory */\n    plg->run = (strategy_fn)dlsym(plg->dl_handle, \"muse_strategy_run\");\n    if (!plg->run) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Plug-in `%s` missing muse_strategy_run symbol\", strategy_name);\n        dlclose(plg->dl_handle);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* optional init/fini */\n    strategy_init_fn init_fn = (strategy_init_fn)dlsym(plg->dl_handle,\n                                                       \"muse_strategy_init\");\n    if (init_fn && init_fn() != 0) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Plug-in `%s` failed to initialize\", strategy_name);\n        dlclose(plg->dl_handle);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    plg->fini = (strategy_fini_fn)dlsym(plg->dl_handle, \"muse_strategy_fini\");\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Loaded strategy `%s`\", strategy_name);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic void plugin_close(plugin_t *plg) {\n    if (!plg || !plg->dl_handle) return;\n    if (plg->fini) plg->fini();\n    dlclose(plg->dl_handle);\n    plg->dl_handle = NULL;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* gRPC Mint-Factory Client (stub)                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int grpc_send_evolution(const char *grpc_target,\n                               const char *recipe_json)\n{\n    /* Placeholder implementation.  In a production build this would use\n     * generated gRPC C bindings to call something like\n     *   MintFactory::EvolveNFT(EvolutionRecipe)\n     */\n    (void)grpc_target; /* suppress unused warning */\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Stub gRPC call sent to Mint-Factory: %s\", recipe_json);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Kafka Consumer                                                            */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic rd_kafka_t *kafka_init_consumer(const muse_cfg_t *cfg)\n{\n    char errstr[512];\n\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(conf, \"bootstrap.servers\",\n                          cfg->kafka_brokers, errstr, sizeof(errstr)) !=\n        RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n        DIE(\"Kafka conf set failed: %s\", errstr);\n\n    /* Auto commit offsets every second */\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(conf, \"enable.auto.commit\",\n                          \"true\", errstr, sizeof(errstr)) != RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n        DIE(\"Kafka conf set failed: %s\", errstr);\n\n    rd_kafka_t *rk = rd_kafka_new(RD_KAFKA_CONSUMER, conf,\n                                  errstr, sizeof(errstr));\n    if (!rk)\n        DIE(\"Failed to create Kafka consumer: %s\", errstr);\n\n    rd_kafka_poll_set_consumer(rk);\n\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *topics =\n        rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_new(1);\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_add(topics, cfg->topic, -1);\n\n    if (rd_kafka_subscribe(rk, topics))\n        DIE(\"Failed to subscribe to %s\", cfg->topic);\n\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_destroy(topics);\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Kafka consumer ready; brokers=%s, topic=%s\",\n        cfg->kafka_brokers, cfg->topic);\n    return rk;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Event Dispatcher                                                          */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int dispatch_event(const muse_cfg_t *cfg,\n                          const char       *event_json,\n                          plugin_t         *cache)\n{\n    /* ---------------------------------\n     * Strategy selection heuristics:\n     *   Input JSON must contain key\n     *     \"strategy\": \"rainbow_shift\"\n     * --------------------------------- */\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loads(event_json, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG(LOG_WARNING, \"Invalid JSON payload: %s\", jerr.text);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    const char *strategy = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"strategy\"));\n    if (!strategy) {\n        LOG(LOG_INFO, \"No strategy specified; ignoring event\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    /* Caching: if last loaded plugin matches, reuse it */\n    plugin_t plg  = {0};\n    plugin_t *use = &plg;\n\n    if (cache && strcmp(cache->name, strategy) == 0 && cache->dl_handle) {\n        use = cache;\n    } else {\n        /* unload previous */\n        if (cache && cache->dl_handle)\n            plugin_close(cache);\n\n        /* load new */\n        if (plugin_open(&plg, cfg->plugin_dir, strategy) != 0) {\n            json_decref(root);\n            return -1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Call strategy */\n    char *recipe_json = NULL;\n    int rc = use->run(event_json, &recipe_json);\n    if (rc != 0 || !recipe_json) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Strategy `%s` failed (rc=%d)\", strategy, rc);\n        json_decref(root);\n        if (use == &plg) plugin_close(&plg);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Send evolution to Mint-Factory */\n    rc = grpc_send_evolution(cfg->grpc_target, recipe_json);\n    free(recipe_json);\n\n    /* Cache plugin for reuse */\n    if (use == &plg && rc == 0 && cache) {\n        *cache = plg; /* shallow copy of handles */\n    } else if (use == &plg) {\n        plugin_close(&plg);\n    }\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Main Event Loop                                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int muse_event_loop(const muse_cfg_t *cfg)\n{\n    rd_kafka_t *consumer = kafka_init_consumer(cfg);\n    int exit_code = EXIT_SUCCESS;\n    plugin_t cache = {0}; /* last-used plugin cache */\n\n    while (!g_terminate) {\n        rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg = rd_kafka_consumer_poll(consumer,\n                                                           POLL_TIMEOUT_MS);\n        if (!rkmsg)\n            continue; /* timeout */\n\n        if (rkmsg->err) {\n            if (rkmsg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n                /* not a real error */\n            } else {\n                LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Kafka error: %s\", rd_kafka_message_errstr(rkmsg));\n            }\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* The payload is not zero-terminated.  Allocate +1 char. */\n        char *payload = malloc(rkmsg->len + 1);\n        if (!payload) DIE(\"malloc failed\");\n        memcpy(payload, rkmsg->payload, rkmsg->len);\n        payload[rkmsg->len] = '\\0';\n\n        if (dispatch_event(cfg, payload, &cache) != 0) {\n            LOG(LOG_WARNING, \"Event processing failed\");\n            /* keep running */\n        }\n\n        free(payload);\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n    }\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Shutting down…\");\n\n    /* Graceful teardown */\n    if (cache.dl_handle)\n        plugin_close(&cache);\n\n    rd_kafka_consumer_close(consumer);\n    rd_kafka_destroy(consumer);\n    rd_kafka_wait_destroyed(2000);\n\n    return exit_code;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Entry Point                                                               */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    openlog(\"holo-muse\", LOG_PID | LOG_CONS, LOG_DAEMON);\n\n    muse_cfg_t cfg = {\n        .kafka_brokers = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_KAFKA_BROKERS\"),\n                                        DEFAULT_BROKERS),\n        .topic         = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_TOPIC\"),      DEFAULT_TOPIC),\n        .plugin_dir    = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_PLUGIN_DIR\"), DEFAULT_PLUGDIR),\n        .grpc_target   = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_GRPC_TARGET\"),DEFAULT_GRPC_TGT),\n        .verbosity     = 0\n    };\n\n    muse_parse_cli(argc, argv, &cfg);\n\n    /* Adjust syslog verbosity */\n    setlogmask(LOG_UPTO(cfg.verbosity ? LOG_DEBUG : LOG_INFO));\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Muse Observer v%s starting…\", MUSE_VERSION);\n\n    /* Install signals */\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = muse_on_signal,\n        .sa_flags   = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n    sigaction(SIGINT,  &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n\n    int rc = muse_event_loop(&cfg);\n\n    free(cfg.kafka_brokers);\n    free(cfg.topic);\n    free(cfg.plugin_dir);\n    free(cfg.grpc_target);\n\n    closelog();\n    return rc;\n}\n```","numLines":420,"startLine":1,"totalLines":420}}}

The wrapper adds:\n *\n *  • Sensible default configuration tuned for low-latency micro-services\n *  • Thread-safe producer / consumer handles\n *  • Centralised error handling & structured logging\n *  • Graceful shutdown hooks\n *\n * The file is implemented header-only to avoid the boiler-plate of a separate\n * compilation unit; simply include it wherever Kafka access is required.\n *\n * NOTE: link with -lrdkafka (and -lpthread on most platforms)\n */\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_H\n#define HC_KAFKA_H\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* System headers                                                            */\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdarg.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Third-party headers                                                       */\n#include <rdkafka/rdkafka.h> /* librdkafka – https://github.com/confluentinc/librdkafka */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Versioning                                                                */\n#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_MAJOR 1\n#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_MINOR 0\n#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_PATCH 0\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Logging                                                                   */\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL\n#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL LOG_INFO\n#endif\n\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_TAG\n#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_TAG \"hc_kafka\"\n#endif\n\ntypedef enum\n{\n    LOG_DEBUG = 0,\n    LOG_INFO,\n    LOG_WARN,\n    LOG_ERROR,\n    LOG_FATAL\n} hc_log_level_e;\n\n/* Simple stdio logger; applications may override by defining\n * HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN(level, fmt, ...) prior to including this header. */\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN\n#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN hc_kafka_default_log\nstatic inline void hc_kafka_default_log(hc_log_level_e level,\n                                        const char *file,\n                                        int line,\n                                        const char *func,\n                                        const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    if (level < HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL)\n        return;\n\n    static const char *LEVEL_STR[] = {\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\", \"FATAL\"};\n\n    char timebuf[64];\n    struct tm tm_now;\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    localtime_r(&ts.tv_sec, &tm_now);\n    strftime(timebuf, sizeof(timebuf), \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\", &tm_now);\n\n    fprintf((level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout,\n            \"%s.%03ld [%s] %s:%d %s() : \",\n            timebuf,\n            ts.tv_nsec / 1000000,\n            LEVEL_STR[level],\n            file,\n            line,\n            func);\n\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    vfprintf((level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n\n    fputc('\\n', (level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout);\n\n    if (level == LOG_FATAL)\n        abort();\n}\n#endif /* HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN */\n\n#define HC_LOG(lvl, ...) HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN((lvl), __FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, __VA_ARGS__)\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Error handling                                                            */\n\ntypedef enum\n{\n    HC_OK = 0,\n    HC_ERR = -1,\n    HC_ERR_TIMEOUT = -2,\n    HC_ERR_EOF = -3\n} hc_err_e;\n\n#define HC_KAFKA_MAX_ERR_STR 512\n\nstatic inline const char *hc_strerror(hc_err_e err)\n{\n    switch (err)\n    {\n    case HC_OK:\n        return \"OK\";\n    case HC_ERR:\n        return \"Generic error\";\n    case HC_ERR_TIMEOUT:\n        return \"Timeout\";\n    case HC_ERR_EOF:\n        return \"End of partition\";\n    default:\n        return \"Unknown error\";\n    }\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Configuration                                                             */\n\n/* The main configuration object that can be shared across producer/consumer. */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    char *bootstrap_servers; /* Use default internal logging */\n\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"bootstrap.servers\", cfg->bootstrap_servers,\n                          self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr)) != RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Kafka conf error: %s\", self->errstr);\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    if (cfg->client_id &&\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"client.id\", cfg->client_id,\n                          self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr)) != RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_WARN, \"Kafka conf client.id: %s\", self->errstr);\n    }\n\n    /* Improve latency for micro-services */\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"queue.buffering.max.ms\", \"5\", NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"linger.ms\", \"0\", NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"acks\", \"all\", NULL, 0);\n\n    self->rk = rd_kafka_new(RD_KAFKA_PRODUCER, self->conf,\n                            self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr));\n    if (!self->rk)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Kafka producer init: %s\", self->errstr);\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_init(&self->lock, NULL);\n    HC_LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Kafka producer created (client.id=%s)\",\n           cfg->client_id ? cfg->client_id : \"N/A\");\n    return self;\n\nfail:\n    if (self->conf)\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(self->conf);\n    free(self);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/*\n * Non-blocking send – copies payload, returns immediately.\n * On success returns HC_OK.  In case of queue‐full backpressure, waits\n * (block_ms) milliseconds before giving up with HC_ERR_TIMEOUT.\n */\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_producer_send(hc_kafka_producer_t *self,\n                       const char *topic,\n                       const void *payload,\n                       size_t len,\n                       int32_t partition, /* RD_KAFKA_PARTITION_UA for any */\n                       int block_ms)\n{\n    if (!self || !topic)\n        return HC_ERR;\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err;\n    int remaining_wait = block_ms;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&self->lock);\n\nretry:\n    err = rd_kafka_producev(\n        self->rk,\n        RD_KAFKA_V_TOPIC(topic),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_PARTITION(partition),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_MSGFLAGS(RD_KAFKA_MSG_F_COPY),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_VALUE(payload, len),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_END);\n\n    if (!err)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&self->lock);\n        return HC_OK; /* queued successfully */\n    }\n\n    if (err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__QUEUE_FULL && remaining_wait > 0)\n    {\n        rd_kafka_poll(self->rk, 0);  /* serve delivery reports */\n        const int backoff = 50;      /* ms */\n        struct timespec ts = {.tv_sec = 0, .tv_nsec = backoff * 1000000};\n        nanosleep(&ts, NULL);\n        remaining_wait -= backoff;\n        goto retry;\n    }\n\n    HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Produce failed: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&self->lock);\n    return HC_ERR;\n}\n\n/* Poll delivery callbacks.  Return number of events served. */\nstatic inline int\nhc_kafka_producer_poll(hc_kafka_producer_t *self, int timeout_ms)\n{\n    return rd_kafka_poll(self->rk, timeout_ms);\n}\n\n/* Flush outstanding messages; timeout_ms < 0 waits indefinitely. */\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_producer_flush(hc_kafka_producer_t *self, int timeout_ms)\n{\n    int ret = rd_kafka_flush(self->rk, timeout_ms < 0 ? INT_MAX : timeout_ms);\n    return (ret == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR_NO_ERROR) ? HC_OK : HC_ERR_TIMEOUT;\n}\n\nstatic inline void\nhc_kafka_producer_destroy(hc_kafka_producer_t *self)\n{\n    if (!self)\n        return;\n\n    hc_kafka_producer_flush(self, 10 * 1000);\n    rd_kafka_destroy(self->rk);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&self->lock);\n    free(self);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Consumer                                                                  */\n\ntypedef void (*hc_kafka_msg_cb)(const rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg, void *opaque);\n\nstruct hc_kafka_consumer_s\n{\n    rd_kafka_t *rk;\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *conf;\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *subscribed;\n    hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg;\n    void *opaque;\n    char errstr[HC_KAFKA_MAX_ERR_STR];\n};\n\nstatic inline hc_kafka_consumer_t *\nhc_kafka_consumer_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *cfg,\n                      const char *group_id,\n                      hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg,\n                      void *opaque)\n{\n    hc_kafka_global_init();\n\n    hc_kafka_consumer_t *self = calloc(1, sizeof(*self));\n    if (!self)\n        return NULL;\n\n    self->conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n                     int timeout_ms,\n                            volatile sig_atomic_t *stop_flag)\n{\n    while (!(*stop_flag))\n    {\n        rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg = Description:\n *      Thin(ish) wrapper around librdkafka that provides the HoloCanvas\n *      micro-services with a sane, opinionated interface for publishing and\n *      consuming protobuf / JSON events on the project-wide Event Bus.\n *\n *  Design notes:\n *      • One producer instance and one consumer instance are multiplexed out of\n *        a single rd_kafka_t handle to conserve file descriptors.\n *      • Thread-safety is provided by an internal mutex guarding the producer’s\n *        shared queue; the consumer runs in the caller’s thread.\n *      • Delivery reports are routed to a ring-buffer so that infrequent callers\n *        can inspect them without registering a full callback.\n *      • All public API symbols are prefixed with “hc_kafka_”.\n *\n *  Build:\n *                         Compile-time Defaults                           */\n\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_PREFIX\n#   define HC_KAFKA_LOG_PREFIX  \"[hc_kafka] \"\n#endif\n\n#ifndef */\n/*                                 Logging                                   */\n\nstatic void hc_kafka_vlog(enum hc_kafka_log_level lvl,\n                          const char *fmt, va_list ap)\n{\n    const char *pfx = HC_KAFKA_LOG_PREFIX;\n    FILE *stream   = (lvl >= HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout;\n\n    flockfile(stream);\n    fprintf(stream, \"%s\", pfx);\n    vfprintf(stream, fmt, ap);\n    fprintf(stream, \"\\n\");\n    funlockfile(stream);\n}\n\nstatic void hc_kafka_log(enum hc_kafka_log_level lvl, const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    hc_kafka_vlog(lvl, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                        Ring-buffer for delivery reports                    */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    hc_kafka_delivery_t buf[HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING];\n    size_t              head;\n    size_t              tail;\n    pthread_mutex_t     mutex;\n} delivery_ring_t;\n\nstatic void ring_init(delivery_ring_t *r)\n{\n    memset(r, 0, sizeof(*r));\n    pthread_mutex_init(&r->mutex, NULL);\n}\n\nstatic void ring_push(delivery_ring_t *r, const hc_kafka_delivery_t *item)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&r->mutex);\n    r->buf[r->head] = *item;\n    r->head = (r->head + 1) % HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING;\n    if (r->head == r->tail) {          /* overwrite oldest */\n        r->tail = (r->tail + 1) % HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING;\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&r->mutex);\n}\n\nstatic int ring_pop(delivery_ring_t *r, hc_kafka_delivery_t *out)\n{\n    int found = 0;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&r->mutex);\n    if (r->tail != r->head) {\n        *out = r->buf[r->tail];\n        r->tail = (r->tail + 1) % HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING;\n        found = 1;\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&r->mutex);\n    return found;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                    Internal structure representing a client               */\n\nstruct hc_kafka_client {\n    rd_kafka_t       *rk;             /* Unified handle (producer & consumer) */\n    rd_kafka_conf_t  *conf;           /* Base configuration object           */\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *subscribed;\n    pthread_mutex_t   producer_mutex; /* Guards produce() and queue length   */\n    delivery_ring_t   d_ring;         /* Ring-buffer for delivery reports    */\n    char             *brokers;\n    char             *client_id;\n    volatile int      run;\n};\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                        librdkafka Callback Shims                           */\n\nstatic void rebalance_cb(rd_kafka_t (hc_kafka_client_t *)opaque;\n    switch (err) {\n        case RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__ASSIGN_PARTITIONS:\n            hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO,\n                         \"Assigned %d partition(s) NULL);\n            break;\n    }\n    (void)cli;\n}\n\nstatic void logger_cb(const rd_kafka_t *rk, int level,\n                      const char *fac, const char *buf)\n{\n    (void)rk; (void)fac;\n    enum hc_kafka_log_level lvl;\n\n    /* Map librdkafka logs to our own */\n    if (level >= 6)\n        lvl = HC_KAFKA_LOG_DEBUG;\n    else if (level >= 4)\n        lvl = HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO;\n    else\n        lvl = HC_KAFKA_LOG_WARN;\n\n    hc_kafka_log(lvl, \"rdkafka: %s\", buf);\n}\n\nstatic void dr_msg_cb(rd_kafka_t *rk,\n                      const rd_kafka_message_t *m,\n hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Delivery failed: %s\",\n                     rd_kafka_err2str(m->err));\n    } else {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_DEBUG,\n                     \"Delivered message to %s [%d] @ %\" PRId64,\n                     d.topic, d.partition, d.offset);\n    }\n    (void)rk;\n}\n\n/* *hc_kafka_client_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *user_conf)\n{\n    hc_kafka_client_t *cli = calloc(1, sizeof(*cli));\n    if (!cli) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR, \"OOM creating dr_msg_cb);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set_rebalance_cb(cli->conf, rebalance_cb);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set_opaque(cli->conf, cli);\n\n    /* Apply caller-provided overrides */\n    if (user_conf->bootstrap_servers)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"bootstrap.servers\",\n                          user_conf->bootstrap_servers, NULL, 0);\n\n    if (user_conf->client_id)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"client.id\",\n                          user_conf->client_id, NULL, 0);\n\n    if (user_conf->group_id)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"group.id\",\n hc_kafka_client_subscribe(hc_kafka_client_t *cli,\n                              const char *const *topics,\n                              size_t topic_count)\n{\n    if (!cli || !topics || topic_count == 0)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *c_conf = rd_kafka_conf_dup(cli->conf);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(c_conf, \"enable.auto.commit\", \"false\", NULL, 0);\n\n    char hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Failed to create consumer: %s\", errstr);\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(c_conf);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_add(cli->subscribed, topics[i],\n                                          RD_KAFKA_PARTITION_UA);\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err =\n        rd_kafka_subscribe(c_consumer, cli->subscribed);\n    if (err) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Subscription failed: %s\",\n                     rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n        rd_kafka_destroy(c_consumer);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Swap the consumer instance into the existing handle */\n    cli->rk = c_consumer;\n    hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO,\n                 \"Subscribed to %zu topic(s)\", topic_count);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_poll(hc_kafka_client_t *cli,\n                         int timeout_ms,\n                         hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg,\n                         void *opaque)\n{\n    if (!cli || !on_msg || timeout_ms < 0)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    rd_kafka_message_t *msg =\n        rd_kafka_consumer_poll(cli->rk, timeout_ms);\n    if (!msg) return 0; /* Timed out */\n\n    if (msg->err) {\n        if (msg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n msg->offset);\n        } else {\n            hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                         \"Consume error: %s\",\n                         rd_kafka_message_errstr(msg));\n        }\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(msg);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    on_msg(msg, opaque);\n    rd_kafka_message_destroy(msg);\n\n    return 1;\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_commit(hc_kafka_client_t *cli)\n{\n    if (!cli) return -EINVAL;\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err = rd_kafka_commit(cli->rk, NULL, 0);\n    if (err) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_WARN,\n                     \"Commit failed: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n        return -1;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_publish(hc_kafka_client_t *cli,\n                            const char *topic,\n                            const char *key,\n                            const void *payload,\n                            size_t len,\n                            int partition,\n                            void *opaque)\n{\n    if (!cli || !topic || !payload)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&cli->producer_mutex);\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err = rd_kafka_producev(\n        cli->rk,\n max_count)\n{\n    if (!cli || !out || max_count == 0)\n        return 0;\n\n    size_t cnt = 0;\n    while (cnt < max_count && ring_pop(&cli->d_ring, &out[cnt]))\n        ++cnt;\n    return cnt;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                           Signal Convenience                              */\n\nstatic volatile int g_should_exit = 0;\n\nstatic void sig_handler(int signo)\n{\n    (void)signo;\n    g_should_exit = 1;\n}\n\nvoid hc_kafka_setup_signal_handlers(void)\n{\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = sig_handler,\n        .sa_flags   = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n    sigaction(SIGINT,  &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n}\n\n/* else()\n        message(WARNING \"IPO/LTO not supported: ${IPO_ERROR}\")\n    endif()\nendif()\n\n# clang-tidy\nif(ENABLE_CLANG_TIDY)\n    find_program(CLANG_TIDY_EXE NAMES clang-tidy)\n \"${PROTO_C_OUT}\")\n\nforeach(proto ${PROTO_FILES})\n    get_filename_component(proto_name ${proto} NAME_WE)\n    add_custom_command(\n        OUTPUT  \"${PROTO_C_OUT}/${proto_name}.pb-c.c\"\n Services can register event handlers\n * for specific event types and the dispatcher will route incoming messages\n * to the appropriate handlers.\n *\n * This module aims to eliminate duplicated boilerplate code across services\n * like governance_hall, mint_factory, and muse_observer.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   1. Create a dispatcher with event_dispatcher_create()\n *   2. Register handlers for event types with event_dispatcher_register_handler()\n *   3. Start the event loop with event_dispatcher_run()\n *   4. Clean up with event_dispatcher_destroy()\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2024 The HoloCanvas Contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#ifndef HC_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n#define HC_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* Include common error types from the shared library */\n#include \"../common/errors.h\"\n\n/* Forward declaration for protobuf-c message base type */\n#ifndef PROTOBUF_C__MESSAGE__DEFINED\ntypedef struct ProtobufCMessage ProtobufCMessage;\n#endif\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Type Definitions\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Function pointer type for event handlers.\n *\n * @param event_type   String identifier for the event type (e.g., \"VOTE_CAST\")\n * @param payload      Pointer to raw JSON payload string\n * @param payload_len  Length of the payload\n * @param user_context User-defined context pointer passed during registration\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, or an appropriate hc_error_t on failure\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                            const char *payload,\n                                            size_t payload_len,\n                                            void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Opaque struct representing the event dispatcher context.\n * The internal implementation is hidden from consumers.\n */\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n/**\n * Configuration structure for creating an event dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;    /**< Comma-separated list of broker addresses */\n    const char *topic;            /**< Kafka topic to subscribe to */\n    const char *group_id;         /**< Consumer group identifier */\n    void *user_context;           /**< User context passed to all handlers */\n    int poll_timeout_ms;          /**< Kafka poll timeout in milliseconds (default: 500) */\n    bool enable_auto_commit;      /**< Enable auto-commit of offsets (default: true) */\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Public API\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher instance.\n *\n * The dispatcher will connect to the specified Kafka brokers and subscribe\n * to the given topic. The user_context will be passed to all registered\n * event handlers.\n *\n * @param config Configuration structure with connection parameters\n *\n * @return Pointer to the newly created dispatcher, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const EventDispatcherConfig *config);\n\n/**\n * Register an event handler for a specific event type.\n *\n * Multiple handlers can be registered for the same event type - they will\n * all be invoked when an event of that type is received.\n *\n * @param dispatcher   Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n * @param event_type   String identifier for the event type (e.g., \"PROPOSAL_CREATED\")\n * @param handler      Function pointer to the event handler\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, or an appropriate error code on failure\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_handler(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                              const char *event_type,\n                                              event_handler_func_t handler);\n\n/**\n * Start the event dispatcher's main consumption loop.\n *\n * This function will block and continuously poll Kafka for messages,\n * deserializing them and dispatching to the appropriate registered handlers.\n * The loop will continue until event_dispatcher_stop() is called or a\n * fatal error occurs.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_run(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Signal the dispatcher to stop its main consumption loop.\n *\n * This function is thread-safe and can be called from a signal handler\n * or another thread. The event_dispatcher_run() function will return\n * after completing any in-progress message processing.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_stop(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Destroy the event dispatcher and release all resources.\n *\n * This function will stop the dispatcher if it's still running,\n * close the Kafka connection, and free all allocated memory including\n * the handler registry.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance (can be NULL)\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_destroy(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Get the user context associated with the dispatcher.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n *\n * @return The user context pointer, or NULL if dispatcher is NULL\n */\nvoid *event_dispatcher_get_context(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Check if the dispatcher is currently running.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n *\n * @return true if running, false otherwise\n */\nbool event_dispatcher_is_running(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Install default signal handlers (SIGINT, SIGTERM) that will stop\n * the given dispatcher.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance to stop on signal\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n} /* extern \"C\" */\n#endif\n\n#endif /* HC_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H */\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":358,"cache_read_input_tokens":110706,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":358,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"65e3dfa4-592f-4ef2-b029-95c5767f99bd","uuid":"891d31a7-1be7-4411-b575-97bdf7a72c98"}

Create a dispatcher with event_dispatcher_create()\n *   2. Register handlers for event types with event_dispatcher_register_handler()\n *   3. Start the event loop with event_dispatcher_run()\n *   4. Clean up with event_dispatcher_destroy()\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2024 The HoloCanvas Contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#ifndef HC_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n#define HC_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* Include common error types from the shared library */\n#include \"../common/errors.h\"\n\n/* Forward declaration for protobuf-c message base type */\n#ifndef PROTOBUF_C__MESSAGE__DEFINED\ntypedef struct ProtobufCMessage ProtobufCMessage;\n#endif\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Type Definitions\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Function pointer type for event handlers.\n *\n * @param event_type   String identifier for the event type (e.g., \"VOTE_CAST\")\n * @param payload      Pointer to raw JSON payload string\n * @param payload_len  Length of the payload\n * @param user_context User-defined context pointer passed during registration\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, or an appropriate hc_error_t on failure\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                            const char *payload,\n                                            size_t payload_len,\n                                            void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Opaque struct representing the event dispatcher context.\n * The internal implementation is hidden from consumers.\n */\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n/**\n * Configuration structure for creating an event dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;    /**< Comma-separated list of broker addresses */\n    const char *topic;            /**< Kafka topic to subscribe to */\n    const char *group_id;         /**< Consumer group identifier */\n    void *user_context;           /**< User context passed to all handlers */\n    int poll_timeout_ms;          /**< Kafka poll timeout in milliseconds (default: 500) */\n    bool enable_auto_commit;      /**< Enable auto-commit of offsets (default: true) */\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Public API\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher instance.\n *\n * The dispatcher will connect to the specified Kafka brokers and subscribe\n * to the given topic. The user_context will be passed to all registered\n * event handlers.\n *\n * @param config Configuration structure with connection parameters\n *\n * @return Pointer to the newly created dispatcher, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const EventDispatcherConfig *config);\n\n/**\n * Register an event handler for a specific event type.\n *\n * Multiple handlers can be registered for the same event type - they will\n * all be invoked when an event of that type is received.\n *\n * @param dispatcher   Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n * @param event_type   String identifier for the event type (e.g., \"PROPOSAL_CREATED\")\n * @param handler      Function pointer to the event handler\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, or an appropriate error code on failure\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_handler(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                              const char *event_type,\n                                              event_handler_func_t handler);\n\n/**\n * Start the event dispatcher's main consumption loop.\n *\n * This function will block and continuously poll Kafka for messages,\n * deserializing them and dispatching to the appropriate registered handlers.\n * The loop will continue until event_dispatcher_stop() is called or a\n * fatal error occurs.\n *\n * @param dispatcher Pointer to the dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_run(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Signal the dispatcher to stop its main consumption loop.\n *\n * This function is thread-safe and can be called from a signal handler\n * or another thread. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01XhBwsQPTdaEoK8XrwbkzBS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012RbnQgo92R9jmi7cNyE7Bc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution_files/shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.c","content":"/**\n * hc_event_dispatcher.c\n *\n * HoloCanvas – Shared Event Dispatcher Implementation\n * ----------------------------------------------------\n * Implementation of the generic event dispatcher that centralizes Kafka\n * event consumption and dispatching logic across HoloCanvas services.\n *\n * Key features:\n *   - Hash map-based handler registry for O(1) event type lookup\n *   - Thread-safe operation with atomic flags for shutdown\n *   - Configurable Kafka consumer settings\n *   - JSON event parsing with type-based routing\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2024 The HoloCanvas Contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#include \"hc_event_dispatcher.h\"\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdatomic.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#include <jansson.h>\n#include <librdkafka/rdkafka.h>\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Internal Constants\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n#define DISPATCHER_LOG_PREFIX \"[EventDispatcher] \"\n#define DEFAULT_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS 500\n#define HANDLER_HASH_SIZE 64  /* Number of hash buckets */\n#define MAX_EVENT_TYPE_LEN 128\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Internal Logging\n * ============================================================================ */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    DISP_LOG_DEBUG = 0,\n    DISP_LOG_INFO,\n    DISP_LOG_WARN,\n    DISP_LOG_ERROR\n} disp_log_level_t;\n\nstatic void disp_log(disp_log_level_t level, const char *fmt, ...) {\n    static const char *LEVEL_STR[] = {\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"};\n\n    char timebuf[32];\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    struct tm tm;\n    localtime_r(&ts.tv_sec, &tm);\n    strftime(timebuf, sizeof(timebuf), \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\", &tm);\n\n    FILE *out = (level >= DISP_LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout;\n\n    fprintf(out, \"%s.%03ld %s%s: \", timebuf, ts.tv_nsec / 1000000,\n            DISPATCHER_LOG_PREFIX, LEVEL_STR[level]);\n\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    vfprintf(out, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n\n    fputc('\\n', out);\n}\n\n#define LOG_DEBUG(...) disp_log(DISP_LOG_DEBUG, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_INFO(...)  disp_log(DISP_LOG_INFO, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_WARN(...)  disp_log(DISP_LOG_WARN, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_ERROR(...) disp_log(DISP_LOG_ERROR, __VA_ARGS__)\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Internal Data Structures\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Entry in the handler linked list for a single hash bucket.\n */\ntypedef struct HandlerEntry {\n    char *event_type;               /**< Event type key (owned) */\n    event_handler_func_t handler;   /**< Handler function pointer */\n    struct HandlerEntry *next;      /**< Next entry in bucket chain */\n} HandlerEntry;\n\n/**\n * Hash map bucket for handler registry.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    HandlerEntry *head;             /**< Head of handler chain */\n} HandlerBucket;\n\n/**\n * Handler registry using a simple hash map.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    HandlerBucket buckets[HANDLER_HASH_SIZE];\n    pthread_rwlock_t lock;          /**< Read-write lock for thread safety */\n    size_t count;                   /**< Total number of registered handlers */\n} HandlerRegistry;\n\n/**\n * Internal dispatcher state.\n */\nstruct EventDispatcher {\n    /* Kafka resources */\n    rd_kafka_t *consumer;\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *kafka_conf;\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *subscribed_topics;\n\n    /* Configuration */\n    char *brokers;\n    char *topic;\n    char *group_id;\n    int poll_timeout_ms;\n\n    /* Handler registry */\n    HandlerRegistry registry;\n\n    /* User context */\n    void *user_context;\n\n    /* Runtime state */\n    atomic_bool running;\n    atomic_bool should_stop;\n};\n\n/* Global dispatcher pointer for signal handler */\nstatic EventDispatcher *g_signal_dispatcher = NULL;\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Hash Map Implementation\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Simple DJB2 hash function for strings.\n */\nstatic unsigned int hash_string(const char *str) {\n    unsigned int hash = 5381;\n    int c;\n    while ((c = *str++)) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) + (unsigned int)c;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nstatic void registry_init(HandlerRegistry *reg) {\n    memset(reg->buckets, 0, sizeof(reg->buckets));\n    pthread_rwlock_init(&reg->lock, NULL);\n    reg->count = 0;\n}\n\nstatic void registry_destroy(HandlerRegistry *reg) {\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < HANDLER_HASH_SIZE; i++) {\n        HandlerEntry *entry = reg->buckets[i].head;\n        while (entry) {\n            HandlerEntry *next = entry->next;\n            free(entry->event_type);\n            free(entry);\n            entry = next;\n        }\n        reg->buckets[i].head = NULL;\n    }\n    reg->count = 0;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&reg->lock);\n    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&reg->lock);\n}\n\nstatic hc_error_t registry_add(HandlerRegistry *reg,\n                               const char *event_type,\n                               event_handler_func_t handler) {\n    if (!event_type || !handler) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_INVALID_ARGUMENT);\n    }\n\n    unsigned int idx = hash_string(event_type) % HANDLER_HASH_SIZE;\n\n    HandlerEntry *entry = malloc(sizeof(HandlerEntry));\n    if (!entry) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n\n    entry->event_type = strdup(event_type);\n    if (!entry->event_type) {\n        free(entry);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n    entry->handler = handler;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    /* Prepend to bucket chain */\n    entry->next = reg->buckets[idx].head;\n    reg->buckets[idx].head = entry;\n    reg->count++;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Find and invoke all handlers for a given event type.\n * Returns the number of handlers invoked.\n */\nstatic int registry_dispatch(HandlerRegistry *reg,\n                             const char *event_type,\n                             const char *payload,\n                             size_t payload_len,\n                             void *user_context) {\n    unsigned int idx = hash_string(event_type) % HANDLER_HASH_SIZE;\n    int count = 0;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    HandlerEntry *entry = reg->buckets[idx].head;\n    while (entry) {\n        if (strcmp(entry->event_type, event_type) == 0) {\n            hc_error_t rc = entry->handler(event_type, payload, payload_len, user_context);\n            if (rc != HC_OK) {\n                LOG_WARN(\"Handler for '%s' returned error: %s\",\n                         event_type, hc_error_str(rc));\n            }\n            count++;\n        }\n        entry = entry->next;\n    }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    return count;\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Kafka Callbacks\n * ============================================================================ */\n\nstatic void kafka_log_cb(const rd_kafka_t *rk, int level,\n                         const char *fac, const char *buf) {\n    (void)rk;\n    (void)fac;\n    if (level <= 3) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"rdkafka: %s\", buf);\n    } else if (level <= 5) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"rdkafka: %s\", buf);\n    } else {\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"rdkafka: %s\", buf);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void kafka_error_cb(rd_kafka_t *rk, int err,\n                           const char *reason, void *opaque) {\n    (void)rk;\n    (void)opaque;\n    LOG_ERROR(\"Kafka error (%d): %s\", err, reason);\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Event Processing\n * ============================================================================ */\n\n/**\n * Parse JSON message and extract event type and payload.\n * Expected format: { \"type\": \"EVENT_TYPE\", \"payload\": {...} }\n */\nstatic hc_error_t process_message(EventDispatcher *disp,\n                                  const void *data,\n                                  size_t len) {\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb((const char *)data, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"JSON parse error at line %d: %s\", jerr.line, jerr.text);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_WARNING, HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT);\n    }\n\n    /* Extract event type */\n    json_t *type_node = json_object_get(root, \"type\");\n    if (!json_is_string(type_node)) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Event missing 'type' field or not a string\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_WARNING, HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT);\n    }\n\n    const char *event_type = json_string_value(type_node);\n\n    /* Extract payload - pass the entire payload subtree as JSON string */\n    json_t *payload_node = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n    char *payload_str = NULL;\n    size_t payload_len = 0;\n\n    if (payload_node) {\n        payload_str = json_dumps(payload_node, JSON_COMPACT);\n        if (payload_str) {\n            payload_len = strlen(payload_str);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* If no payload, use the raw message */\n    if (!payload_str) {\n        payload_str = strndup((const char *)data, len);\n        payload_len = len;\n    }\n\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"Processing event: type=%s, payload_len=%zu\", event_type, payload_len);\n\n    /* Dispatch to registered handlers */\n    int handler_count = registry_dispatch(&disp->registry,\n                                          event_type,\n                                          payload_str,\n                                          payload_len,\n                                          disp->user_context);\n\n    if (handler_count == 0) {\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"No handlers registered for event type '%s'\", event_type);\n    } else {\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"Dispatched '%s' to %d handler(s)\", event_type, handler_count);\n    }\n\n    free(payload_str);\n    json_decref(root);\n\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Signal Handling\n * ============================================================================ */\n\nstatic void dispatcher_signal_handler(int sig) {\n    (void)sig;\n    if (g_signal_dispatcher) {\n        event_dispatcher_stop(g_signal_dispatcher);\n    }\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Public API Implementation\n * ============================================================================ */\n\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const EventDispatcherConfig *config) {\n    if (!config || !config->kafka_brokers || !config->topic || !config->group_id) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Invalid configuration: missing required parameters\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    EventDispatcher *disp = calloc(1, sizeof(EventDispatcher));\n    if (!disp) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to allocate dispatcher\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Copy configuration */\n    disp->brokers = strdup(config->kafka_brokers);\n    disp->topic = strdup(config->topic);\n    disp->group_id = strdup(config->group_id);\n    disp->poll_timeout_ms = config->poll_timeout_ms > 0\n                            ? config->poll_timeout_ms\n                            : DEFAULT_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS;\n    disp->user_context = config->user_context;\n\n    if (!disp->brokers || !disp->topic || !disp->group_id) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to copy configuration strings\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n\n    /* Initialize handler registry */\n    registry_init(&disp->registry);\n\n    /* Configure Kafka consumer */\n    char errstr[512];\n    disp->kafka_conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(disp->kafka_conf, \"bootstrap.servers\",\n                          disp->brokers, errstr, Logging\n * ============================================================================ */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    DISP_LOG_DEBUG = 0,\n    DISP_LOG_INFO,\n    DISP_LOG_WARN,\n    DISP_LOG_ERROR\n} disp_log_level_t;\n\nstatic void disp_log(disp_log_level_t level, const char *fmt, ...) {\n    static const char *LEVEL_STR[] = {\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"};\n\n    char timebuf[32];\n   va_start(ap, fmt);\n    vfprintf(out, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n\n    fputc('\\n', out);\n}\n\n#define LOG_DEBUG(...) disp_log(DISP_LOG_DEBUG, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_INFO(...) int hash = 5381;\n    int c;\n    while ((c = *str++)) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) + (unsigned int)c;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nstatic void registry_init(HandlerRegistry *reg) {\n    memset(reg->buckets, 0, sizeof(reg->buckets));\n    pthread_rwlock_init(&reg->lock, NULL);\n    reg->count = 0;\n}\n\nstatic void registry_destroy(HandlerRegistry *reg) {\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < HANDLER_HASH_SIZE; i++) {\n        HandlerEntry *entry = reg->buckets[i].head;\n        while (entry) {\n            HandlerEntry *next =                       event_handler_func_t handler) {\n    if (!event_type || !handler) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_INVALID_ARGUMENT);\n    }\n\n    unsigned number of handlers invoked.\n */\nstatic int registry_dispatch(HandlerRegistry *reg,\n                             const char *event_type,\n                             const char *payload,\n                             size_t payload_len,\n                             void *user_context) {\n    unsigned int idx = hash_string(event_type) % HANDLER_HASH_SIZE;\n    int count = 0;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&reg->lock);\n\n    HandlerEntry *entry = reg->buckets[idx].head;\n    while (entry) {\n        if (strcmp(entry->event_type, event_type) == 0) {\n            hc_error_t rc = kafka_log_cb(const rd_kafka_t *rk, int level,\n                         const char *fac, const char *buf) {\n    (void)rk;\n    (void)fac;\n    if (level <= 3) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"rdkafka: %s\",                 const void *data,\n                                  size_t len) {\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb((const char *)data, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"JSON parse error at line %d: %s\", jerr.line, jerr.text);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_WARNING, HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT);\n    }\n\n    /* Extract event type */\n    json_t *type_node = json_object_get(root, \"type\");\n    if (!json_is_string(type_node)) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Event missing 'type' field or not a string\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_WARNING, HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT);\n    }\n\n    const char *event_type = json_string_value(type_node);\n\n    /* Extract payload - pass the entire payload subtree as JSON string */\n    json_t *payload_node = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n    char *payload_str = NULL;\n    size_t payload_len = 0;\n\n    if (payload_node) {\n        payload_str = json_dumps(payload_node, JSON_COMPACT);\n        if (payload_str) {\n            payload_len = strlen(payload_str);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* If no payload, use the raw message */\n    if (!payload_str) {\n        payload_str = strndup((const char *)data, len);\n        payload_len = len;\n    }\n\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"Processing event: type=%s, payload_len=%zu\", event_type, payload_len);\n\n    /* Dispatch to registered handlers */\n    int handler_count = registry_dispatch(&disp->registry,\n                                          event_type,\n                                          payload_str,\n                                          payload_len,\n                                          disp->user_context);\n\n    if (handler_count == 0) {\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"No handlers registered for event type '%s'\", event_type);\n    } else {\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"Dispatched '%s' to %d handler(s)\", event_type, handler_count);\n    }\n\n    free(payload_str);\n    json_decref(root);\n\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Signal Handling\n * ============================================================================ */\n\nstatic void dispatcher_signal_handler(int sig) {\n    (void)sig;\n    if (g_signal_dispatcher) {\n }\n\n    if (atomic_load(&dispatcher->running)) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Dispatcher is already running\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    atomic_store(&dispatcher->running, true);\n     rd_kafka_destroy(dispatcher->consumer);\n    }\n\n    if (dispatcher->kafka_conf) {\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(dispatcher->kafka_conf);\n    }\n\n    if (dispatcher->subscribed_topics) {\n (lvl == LOG_FATAL) {\n        abort();\n    }\n}\n\n#define LOGD(...) log_internal(LOG_DEBUG, __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGI(...) log_internal(LOG_INFO,  __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGW(...) log_internal(LOG_WARN,  __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGE(...) log_internal(LOG_ERROR, __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGF(...)                    */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    PROPOSAL_DRAFT = 0,\n    PROPOSAL_VOTING,\n    PROPOSAL_ACCEPTED,\n }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_prop_lock);\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_proposals, id, p);\n    if (!p) {\n        p = calloc(1, sizeof *p);\n        if (!p) {\n pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_prop_lock);\n    return p;\n}\n\nstatic void proposal_try_finalize(governance_proposal_t *p)\n{\n    if (p->state != PROPOSAL_VOTING) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n    if (now < p->voting_deadline) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    p->state = (p->yes_votes > p->no_votes)\n                 ? PROPOSAL_ACCEPTED\n                 : PROPOSAL_REJECTED;\n\n    LOGI(\"Proposal %s finalized as %s (yes=%\" PRIu64 \", no=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n         p->id,\n         (p->state == PROPOSAL_ACCEPTED) ? \"ACCEPTED\" : \"REJECTED\",\n         p->yes_votes, p->no_votes);\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                     Event Handlers (Using New Signature)                   */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Handler for PROPOSAL_CREATE events.\n */\nhc_error_t json_decref(root);\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Handler for VOTE_CAST events.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_vote_cast(const char *event_type,\n                             const char *payload,\n                             size_t payload_len,\n                             void *user_context)\n{\n    (void)event_type;\n    (void)payload_len;\n    (void)user_context;\n\n    json_error_t jerr;\n \"yes\") == 0);\n    if (vote_yes)\n        p->yes_votes++;\n    else\n        p->no_votes++;\n\n    LOGD(\"Vote registered on %s → yes=%\" PRIu64 \", no=%\" PRIu64,\n         p->id, p->yes_votes, (strategy_fini_fn)dlsym(plg->dl_handle, \"muse_strategy_fini\");\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Loaded strategy `%s`\", strategy_name);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic void plugin_close(plugin_t *plg) {\n    if (!plg || !plg->dl_handle) return;\n    if (plg->fini) plg->fini();\n    dlclose(plg->dl_handle);\n    plg->dl_handle = NULL;\n    memset(plg->name, 0, sizeof(plg->name));\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* gRPC Mint-Factory Client (stub)                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int grpc_send_evolution(const char *grpc_target, const char *recipe_json)\n{\n    (void)grpc_target;\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Stub gRPC call sent to Mint-Factory: %s\", recipe_json);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Service Context                                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    muse_cfg_t *cfg;\n    plugin_t   *plugin_cache;\n} muse_context_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Event Handlers (New Dispatcher API)                                       */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Generic event handler that dispatches to the appropriate strategy plugin.\n * The strategy is selected based on the \"strategy\" field in the JSON payload.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_muse_event(const char *event_type,\n                              const char *payload,\n                              size_t payload_len,\n                              void *user_context)\n{\n    (void)event_type;\n    (void)payload_len;\n\n    muse_context_t *ctx = (muse_context_t *)user_context;\n    if (!ctx || !ctx->cfg) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_INVALID_ARGUMENT);\n    }\n\n    /* Parse JSON to find strategy */\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loads(payload, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG(LOG_WARNING, \"Invalid JSON payload: %s\", jerr.text);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_WARNING, HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT);\n    }\n\n    const char *strategy = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"strategy\"));\n    if (!strategy) {\n        LOG(LOG_INFO, \"No strategy specified; ignoring event\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return HC_OK;\n    }\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Received event type '%s' with strategy '%s'\", event_type, strategy);\n\n    /* Caching: if last loaded plugin matches, reuse it */\n    plugin_t plg = {0};\n    plugin_t *use = &plg;\n\n    if (ctx->plugin_cache &&\n        strcmp(ctx->plugin_cache->name, strategy) == 0 &&\n        ctx->plugin_cache->dl_handle) {\n        use = ctx->plugin_cache;\n    } else {\n        /* Unload previous */\n        if (ctx->plugin_cache && ctx->plugin_cache->dl_handle)\n            plugin_close(ctx->plugin_cache);\n\n        /* Load new */\n        if (plugin_open(&plg, ctx->cfg->plugin_dir, strategy) != 0) {\n            json_decref(root);\n            return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_DEPENDENCY_FAILURE);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Call strategy */\n    char *recipe_json = NULL;\n    int rc = use->run(payload, &recipe_json);\n    if (rc != 0 || !recipe_json) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Strategy `%s` failed (rc=%d)\", strategy, rc);\n        json_decref(root);\n        if (use == &plg) plugin_close(&plg);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_DEPENDENCY_FAILURE);\n    }\n\n    /* Send evolution to Mint-Factory */\n    rc = grpc_send_evolution(ctx->cfg->grpc_target, recipe_json);\n    free(recipe_json);\n\n    /* Cache plugin for reuse */\n    if (use == &plg && rc == 0 && ctx->plugin_cache) {\n        *ctx->plugin_cache = plg;\n    } else if (use == &plg) {\n        plugin_close(&plg);\n    }\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return rc == 0 ? HC_OK : HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_NETWORK_UNREACHABLE);\n}\n\n/**\n * Handler for ARTISTIC_TRIGGER events.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_artistic_trigger(const char *event_type,\n                                    const char *payload,\n                                    size_t payload_len,\n                                    void *user_context)\n{\n    return handle_muse_event(event_type, payload, payload_len, user_context);\n}\n\n/**\n * Handler for ORACLE_UPDATE events.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_oracle_update(const char *event_type,\n                                 const char *payload,\n                                 size_t payload_len,\n                                 void *user_context)\n{\n    return handle_muse_event(event_type, payload, payload_len, user_context);\n}\n\n/**\n * Handler for DAO_VOTE events.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_dao_vote(const char *event_type,\n                            const char *payload,\n                            size_t payload_len,\n                            void *user_context)\n{\n    return handle_muse_event(event_type, payload, payload_len, user_context);\n}\n\n/**\n * Handler for LIKE_RECEIVED events.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_like_received(const char *event_type,\n                                 const char *payload,\n                                 size_t payload_len,\n                                 void *user_context)\n{\n    return handle_muse_event(event_type, payload, payload_len, user_context);\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Entry Point                                                               */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    openlog(\"holo-muse\", LOG_PID | LOG_CONS, LOG_DAEMON);\n\n    muse_cfg_t cfg = {\n        .kafka_brokers = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_KAFKA_BROKERS\"), DEFAULT_BROKERS),\n        .topic         = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_TOPIC\"), DEFAULT_TOPIC),\n        .group_id      = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_GROUP_ID\"), DEFAULT_GROUP_ID),\n        .plugin_dir    = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_PLUGIN_DIR\"), DEFAULT_PLUGDIR),\n        .grpc_target   = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_GRPC_TARGET\"), DEFAULT_GRPC_TGT),\n        .verbosity     = 0\n    };\n\n    muse_parse_cli(argc, argv, &cfg);\n\n    /* Adjust syslog verbosity */\n    setlogmask(LOG_UPTO(cfg.verbosity ? LOG_DEBUG : LOG_INFO));\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Muse Observer v%s starting...\", MUSE_VERSION);\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Config: brokers=%s, topic=%s, group=%s\",\n        cfg.kafka_brokers, cfg.topic, cfg.group_id);\n\n    /* Create service context */\n    muse_context_t muse_ctx = {\n        .cfg = &cfg,\n        .plugin_cache = &g_plugin_cache\n    };\n\n    /* Create the event dispatcher */\n    EventDispatcherConfig disp_config = {\n        .kafka_brokers = cfg.kafka_brokers,\n        .topic = cfg.topic,\n        .group_id = cfg.group_id,\n        .user_context = &muse_ctx,\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 500,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&disp_config);\n    if (!dispatcher) {\n        DIE(\"Failed to create event dispatcher\");\n    }\n\n    /* Register event handlers for all event types we care about */\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"ARTISTIC_TRIGGER\", handle_artistic_trigger);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"ORACLE_UPDATE\", handle_oracle_update);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"DAO_VOTE\", handle_dao_vote);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"LIKE_RECEIVED\", handle_like_received);\n\n    /* Install signal handlers */\n    event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(dispatcher);\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Muse Observer ready, starting event loop...\");\n\n    /* Run the event loop (blocks until stopped) */\n    event_dispatcher_run(dispatcher);\n\n    /* Cleanup */\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Shutting down...\");\n\n    if (g_plugin_cache.dl_handle)\n        plugin_close(&g_plugin_cache);\n\n    event_dispatcher_destroy(dispatcher);\n\n    free(cfg.kafka_brokers);\n    free(cfg.topic);\n    free(cfg.group_id);\n    free(cfg.plugin_dir);\n    free(cfg.grpc_target);\n\n    closelog();\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Muse Observer terminated gracefully\");\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
