{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01Jqv92SFcQB9q6ypR59arxU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VUnLGP8U29bjk4eBahjjZh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# HoloCanvas Event Dispatcher Refactoring Solution\n\n## Overview\n\nThis document describes the complete solution for refactoring the disparate event handling logic across the HoloCanvas microservices into a shared, generic Event Dispatcher module. The goal is to centralize the duplicated Kafka consumption, message deserialization, and event dispatching code currently spread across `governance_hall`, `mint_factory`, and `muse_observer` services.\n\n## Problem Analysis\n\n### Current State\n\nAfter analyzing the codebase, I identified the following duplicated patterns across the three services:\n\n1. **`governance_hall/src/event_handler.c`** (lines 217-435):\n   - Kafka consumer initialization with `rd_kafka_new()`, `rd_kafka_conf_set()`, topic subscription\n   - Consumer loop in `consumer_loop()` function (lines 343-404)\n   - JSON parsing with jansson (`json_loadb()`)\n   - Event type extraction and dispatch via callback linked list\n   - Signature verification (service-specific)\n\n2. **`mint_factory/src/event_handler.c`** (lines 119-249):\n   - Similar Kafka setup in `evh_init()` (lines 119-174)\n   - Consumer loop in `run_loop()` (lines 201-249)\n   - JSON parsing to extract event type and payload\n   - Switch-based dispatch in `dispatch_event()` (lines 331-365)\n\n3. **`muse_observer/src/event_listener.c`** (lines 152-342):\n   - Kafka consumer creation in `event_listener_create()` (lines 152-210)\n   - Consumer loop in `listener_thread()` (lines 269-307)\n   - JSON parsing and dispatch in `dispatch_event()` (lines 311-342)\n\n### Common Pattern Identified\n\nAll three services follow this pattern:\n1. Initialize Kafka consumer with brokers, group_id, and topic\n2. Subscribe to topic(s)\n3. Run a polling loop that:\n   - Polls Kafka for messages\n   - Parses JSON to extract event type\n   - Routes to appropriate handler based on type\n4. Handle graceful shutdown\n\n## Solution Design\n\n### New Module: `shared/event_dispatcher`\n\nCreated a new shared module with two files:\n\n#### `hc_event_dispatcher.h` - Public Interface\n\n```c\n// Function pointer type for event handlers\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                            const char *payload,\n                                            size_t payload_len,\n                                            void *user_context);\n\n// Opaque struct for the event dispatcher context\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n// Configuration structure\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;\n    const char *topic;\n    const char *group_id;\n    void *user_context;\n    int poll_timeout_ms;\n    bool enable_auto_commit;\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n// Public API\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const EventDispatcherConfig *config);\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_handler(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                              const char *event_type,\n                                              event_handler_func_t handler);\nvoid event_dispatcher_run(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid event_dispatcher_stop(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid event_dispatcher_destroy(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid *event_dispatcher_get_context(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nbool event_dispatcher_is_running(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n```\n\n#### `hc_event_dispatcher.c` - Implementation\n\nKey implementation details:\n\n1. **Hash Map Registry**: Uses a DJB2 hash-based registry with 64 buckets for O(1) handler lookup:\n   ```c\n   #define HANDLER_HASH_SIZE 64\n\n   typedef struct HandlerEntry {\n       char *event_type;\n       event_handler_func_t handler;\n       struct HandlerEntry *next;\n   } HandlerEntry;\n\n   typedef struct {\n       HandlerEntry *head;\n   } HandlerBucket;\n   ```\n\n2. **Thread Safety**:\n   - `pthread_rwlock_t` for the handler registry (allows concurrent reads, exclusive writes)\n   - `atomic_bool` for running/should_stop flags\n\n3. **Kafka Integration**:\n   - Uses librdkafka consumer API\n   - Configurable poll timeout\n   - Auto-commit or manual commit support\n\n4. **JSON Processing**:\n   - Uses jansson library for JSON parsing\n   - Extracts `type` field for routing\n   - Extracts `payload` field for handler\n\n5. **Resource Management**:\n   - Clean destruction of all resources in `event_dispatcher_destroy()`\n   - Signal handler installation for graceful shutdown\n\n## Refactored Services\n\n### 1. `governance_hall/src/main.c`\n\n**Changes Made:**\n- Removed: Direct Kafka consumer initialization and loop in `event_consumer_thread()`\n- Removed: Local event parsing and dispatch logic\n- Added: EventDispatcher initialization and handler registration\n\n**New Structure:**\n```c\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    // ... config loading ...\n\n    // Create dispatcher\n    EventDispatcherConfig config = {\n        .kafka_brokers = brokers,\n        .topic = topic,\n        .group_id = group_id,\n        .user_context = NULL,\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 500,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&config);\n\n    // Register handlers\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"PROPOSAL_CREATE\", handle_proposal_create);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"VOTE_CAST\", handle_vote_cast);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"FINALIZE_REQ\", handle_finalize_request);\n\n    // Install signal handlers and run\n    event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(dispatcher);\n    event_dispatcher_run(dispatcher);\n\n    // Cleanup\n    event_dispatcher_destroy(dispatcher);\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n**Handler Signature Update:**\n```c\nhc_error_t handle_proposal_create(const char *event_type,\n                                   const char *payload,\n                                   size_t payload_len,\n                                   void *user_context);\n```\n\n### 2. `mint_factory/src/main.c`\n\n**Changes Made:**\n- Removed: `evh_init()` and `run_loop()` Kafka handling\n- Removed: Local `dispatch_event()` switch statement\n- Added: Service context struct for passing queue and config to handlers\n- Added: EventDispatcher with handlers for MINT_REQUEST, BID, GOVERNANCE_VOTE, ORACLE_FEED\n\n**New Structure:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    bqueue_t *queue;\n    config_t *cfg;\n} service_context_t;\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    // ... setup ...\n\n    service_context_t svc_ctx = {\n        .queue = &queue,\n        .cfg = &cfg\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcherConfig disp_config = {\n        .kafka_brokers = cfg.kafka_broker,\n        .topic = cfg.kafka_topic,\n        .group_id = cfg.kafka_group_id,\n        .user_context = &svc_ctx,  // Pass service context\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 250,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&disp_config);\n\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"MINT_REQUEST\", handle_mint_request);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"BID\", handle_bid);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"GOVERNANCE_VOTE\", handle_governance_vote);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"ORACLE_FEED\", handle_oracle_feed);\n\n    // ... worker threads still managed separately ...\n\n    event_dispatcher_run(dispatcher);\n\n    // ... cleanup ...\n}\n```\n\n### 3. `muse_observer/src/main.c`\n\n**Changes Made:**\n- Removed: `kafka_init_consumer()` and `muse_event_loop()` functions\n- .user_context = NULL,\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 500,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&config);\n\n The goal is to centralize the duplicated Kafka consumption, message deserialization, and event dispatching code currently spread across `governance_hall`, `mint_factory`, and `muse_observer` services.\n\n## Problem Analysis\n\n### Current State\n\nAfter analyzing the codebase, I identified the following duplicated patterns across the three services:\n\n1. **`governance_hall/src/event_handler.c`** (lines 217-435):\n   - Kafka consumer initialization with `rd_kafka_new()`, `rd_kafka_conf_set()`, topic subscription\n   - Consumer loop in `consumer_loop()` function (lines 343-404)\n   - JSON parsing with jansson (`json_loadb()`)\n   - Event type extraction and dispatch via callback linked list\n   - Signature verification (service-specific)\n\n2. **`mint_factory/src/event_handler.c`** (lines 119-249):\n   - Similar Kafka setup in `evh_init()` (lines 119-174)\n   - Consumer loop in `run_loop()` (lines 201-249)\n   - JSON parsing to extract event type and payload\n   - Switch-based dispatch in `dispatch_event()` (lines 331-365)\n\n3. **`muse_observer/src/event_listener.c`** (lines 152-342):\n   - Kafka consumer creation in `event_listener_create()` (lines 152-210)\n   - Consumer loop in `listener_thread()` (lines 269-307)\n   - JSON parsing and dispatch in `dispatch_event()` (lines 311-342)\n\n### Common Pattern Identified\n\nAll three services follow this pattern:\n1. Initialize Kafka consumer with brokers, group_id, and topic\n2. Subscribe to topic(s)\n3. Run a polling loop that:\n   - Polls Kafka for messages\n   - Parses JSON to extract event type\n   - Routes to appropriate handler based on type\n4. Handle graceful shutdown\n\n## Solution Design\n\n### New Module: `shared/event_dispatcher`\n\nCreated a new shared module with two files:\n\n#### `hc_event_dispatcher.h` - Public Interface\n\n```c\n// Function pointer type for event handlers\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                            const char *payload,\n                                            size_t payload_len,\n                                            void *user_context);\n\n// Opaque struct for the event dispatcher context\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n// Configuration structure\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;\n    const char *topic;\n    const char *group_id;\n    void *user_context;\n    int poll_timeout_ms;\n    bool enable_auto_commit;\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n// Public API\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const EventDispatcherConfig *config);\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_handler(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                              const char *event_type,\n                                              event_handler_func_t handler);\nvoid event_dispatcher_run(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid event_dispatcher_stop(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid event_dispatcher_destroy(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid *event_dispatcher_get_context(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nbool event_dispatcher_is_running(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\nvoid event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n```\n\n#### `hc_event_dispatcher.c` - Implementation\n\nKey implementation details:\n\n1. **Hash Map Registry**: Uses a DJB2 hash-based registry with 64 buckets for O(1) handler lookup:\n   ```c\n   #define HANDLER_HASH_SIZE 64\n\n   typedef struct HandlerEntry {\n       char *event_type;\n       event_handler_func_t handler;\n       struct HandlerEntry *next;\n   } HandlerEntry;\n\n   typedef struct {\n       HandlerEntry *head;\n   } HandlerBucket;\n   ```\n\n2. **Thread Safety**:\n   - `pthread_rwlock_t` for the handler registry (allows concurrent reads, exclusive writes)\n   - `atomic_bool` for running/should_stop flags\n\n3. **Kafka Integration**:\n   - Uses librdkafka consumer API\n   - Configurable poll timeout\n   - Auto-commit or manual commit support\n\n4. **JSON Processing**:\n   - Uses jansson library for JSON parsing\n   - Extracts `type` field for routing\n   - Extracts `payload` field for handler\n\n5. **Resource Management**:\n   - Clean destruction of all resources in `event_dispatcher_destroy()`\n   - Signal handler installation for graceful shutdown\n\n## Refactored Services\n\n### 1. `governance_hall/src/main.c`\n\n**Changes Made:**\n- Removed: Direct Kafka consumer initialization and loop in `event_consumer_thread()`\n- Removed: Local event parsing and dispatch logic\n- Added: EventDispatcher initialization and handler registration\n\n**New Structure:**\n```c\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    // ... config loading ...\n\n    // Create dispatcher\n    EventDispatcherConfig config = {\n        .kafka_brokers = brokers,\n        .topic = topic,\n        .group_id = group_id,\n        .user_context = NULL,\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 500,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&config);\n\n    // Register handlers\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"PROPOSAL_CREATE\", handle_proposal_create);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"VOTE_CAST\", handle_vote_cast);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"FINALIZE_REQ\", handle_finalize_request);\n\n    // Install signal handlers and run\n    event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(dispatcher);\n    event_dispatcher_run(dispatcher);\n\n    // Cleanup\n    event_dispatcher_destroy(dispatcher);\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n**Handler Signature Update:**\n```c\nhc_error_t handle_proposal_create(const char *event_type,\n                                   const char *payload,\n                                   size_t payload_len,\n                                   void *user_context);\n```\n\n### 2. `mint_factory/src/main.c`\n\n**Changes Made:**\n- Removed: `evh_init()` and `run_loop()` Kafka handling\n- Removed: Local `dispatch_event()` switch statement\n- Added: Service context struct for passing queue and config to handlers\n- Added: EventDispatcher with handlers for MINT_REQUEST, BID, GOVERNANCE_VOTE, ORACLE_FEED\n\n**New Structure:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    bqueue_t *queue;\n    config_t *cfg;\n} service_context_t;\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    // ... setup ...\n\n    service_context_t svc_ctx = {\n        .queue = &queue,\n        .cfg = &cfg\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcherConfig disp_config = {\n        .kafka_brokers = cfg.kafka_broker,\n        .topic = cfg.kafka_topic,\n        .group_id = cfg.kafka_group_id,\n        .user_context = &svc_ctx,  // Pass service context\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 250,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&disp_config);\n\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"MINT_REQUEST\", handle_mint_request);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"BID\", handle_bid);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"GOVERNANCE_VOTE\", handle_governance_vote);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"ORACLE_FEED\", handle_oracle_feed);\n\n    // ... worker threads still managed separately ...\n\n    event_dispatcher_run(dispatcher);\n\n    // ... cleanup ...\n}\n```\n\n### 3. `muse_observer/src/main.c`\n\n**Changes Made:**\n- Removed: `kafka_init_consumer()` and `muse_event_loop()` functions\n- Removed: Local Kafka polling and message handling\n- Added: Service context struct with config and plugin cache\n- Added: EventDispatcher with handlers for ARTISTIC_TRIGGER, ORACLE_UPDATE, DAO_VOTE, LIKE_RECEIVED\n\n**New Structure:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    muse_cfg_t *cfg;\n    plugin_t   *plugin_cache;\n} muse_context_t;\n\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    // ... config loading ...\n\n    muse_context_t muse_ctx = {\n        .cfg = &cfg,\n        .plugin_cache = &g_plugin_cache\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcherConfig disp_config = {\n        .kafka_brokers = cfg.kafka_brokers,\n        .topic = cfg.topic,\n        .group_id = cfg.group_id,\n        .user_context = &muse_ctx,\n        .poll_timeout_ms = 500,\n        .enable_auto_commit = true\n    };\n\n    EventDispatcher *dispatcher = event_dispatcher_create(&disp_config);\n\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"ARTISTIC_TRIGGER\", handle_artistic_trigger);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"ORACLE_UPDATE\", handle_oracle_update);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"DAO_VOTE\", handle_dao_vote);\n    event_dispatcher_register_handler(dispatcher, \"LIKE_RECEIVED\", handle_like_received);\n\n    event_dispatcher_run(dispatcher);\n\n    // ... cleanup ...\n}\n```\n\n## Build System Updates\n\n###
